=== givingback mechanical engineering dept ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/mechanical-engineering-dept.php
Department of Mechanical Engineering
Indian Institute of Technology Kanpur
The department of Mechanical Engineering at IIT Kanpur aspires to be a global leader in engineering education, research, and innovation. Built on a strong foundation in the mechanical sciences, the department is committed to advancing cutting-edge research, transformative technologies, and impactful engineering solutions. The department aims to play a pivotal role in shaping the next generation of engineering innovation and strengthening the country's advanced industrial ecosystem.
To realise this vision, we seek the support of our alumni, industry partners, and well-wishers. The following initiatives are designed to catalyse excellence in education, research, and technological innovation.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback mechanical engineering dept]
1
ME DEPARTMENT EXCELLENCE FUND
The ME Department Excellence Fund will support activities that enrich the academic and intellectual environment of the department. Contributions to this fund will enable:
Student travel awards to present research at leading international conferences
Outreach initiatives that inspire the next generation of engineers
Seminars and workshops with global experts
Student excellence awards recognising outstanding achievements
An International Visitors Program to bring distinguished scholars and industry leaders to the department
IIT Kanpur provides a matching grant for contributions made to this fund, amplifying the impact of donor support.
This is a current-use fund that will directly benefit students and academic activities.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback mechanical engineering dept]
DONATE NOW
2
EARLY CAREER FACULTY CATALYST FUND
Attracting and nurturing exceptional faculty is essential for sustaining academic excellence. The Early Career Faculty Catalyst Fund will provide top-up research support for newly recruited faculty members, enabling them to initiate ambitious research programs during the early stages of their careers. This support helps young academicians build momentum, develop innovative ideas, and secure major research grants from national and international funding agencies.
This is a current-use fund.
DONATE NOW
3
FRONTIERS OF INNOVATION RESEARCH FUND
Breakthrough innovations often begin with early-stage ideas that require initial support to demonstrate feasibility. The Frontiers of Innovation Research Fund will provide resources for faculty members to develop proof-of-concept prototypes and exploratory research ideas. These efforts will help accelerate innovation, raise Technology Readiness Levels (TRLs), and create technologies that can attract substantial support from research agencies and industry partners.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback mechanical engineering dept]
This fund aims to transform bold ideas into impactful technologies.
This is a current-use fund.
DONATE NOW
4
UNDERGRADUATE DISCOVERY AND RESEARCH FUND
Undergraduate research is a powerful way to inspire curiosity and creativity among young engineers. The Undergraduate Discovery and Research Fund will support student-led research projects, design initiatives, and innovation activities. It will also contribute to the modernisation and upgrading of undergraduate laboratories, ensuring that students gain hands-on exposure to cutting-edge tools and technologies.
By supporting early research experiences, this initiative will help cultivate the next generation of innovators in mechanical engineering.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback mechanical engineering dept]
This is a current-use fund.
DONATE NOW
5
GRADUATE RESEARCH AND INNOVATION FUND
Postgraduate students play a vital role in advancing research and developing technologies with real-world impact. The Graduate Research and Innovation Fund will support graduate-level research projects and translational research activities, helping students bridge the gap between academic discovery and industrial application. The fund will also support the upgradation of advanced research laboratories, ensuring that our students work with modern tools and facilities aligned with global standards.
This is a current-use fund.
DONATE NOW
6
SAMEER KHANDEKAR MEMORIAL LECTURE SERIES – FUNDRAISING APPEAL
The Department of Mechanical Engineering at IIT Kanpur proposes to institute the Sameer Khandekar Memorial Lecture Series in honor of our beloved colleague, Prof. Sameer Khandekar, whose untimely passing in December 2023 left an irreplaceable void in our academic community.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback mechanical engineering dept]
7
LEGACY ENDOWMENTS AND NAMED GIVING OPPORTUNITIES
Donors may also support the department through endowed gifts that create a lasting legacy. Possible endowment opportunities include:
Named scholarships for deserving students
Named faculty chair positions to attract and retain world-class faculty
Named infrastructure and laboratory facilities within the department
Memorial funds honouring beloved teachers, mentors, or colleagues
Batch endowments created by alumni groups to celebrate shared legacy
Endowments ensure that the impact of your contribution continues to benefit future generations of students and researchers.
Please Contact Prof. Anurag Gupta head_me@iitk.ac.in if you are interested in creating any of these.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback mechanical engineering dept]
PARTNER WITH US
We warmly invite alumni, industry leaders, and friends of the department to join us in strengthening the future of mechanical engineering at IIT Kanpur.
Your support can help empower students, enable groundbreaking research, and build the technologies that will shape the future of industry and society.
We also welcome new ideas and suggestions on how alumni and partners can contribute to the continued growth and excellence of the department.
PLEASE FEEL FREE TO CONTACT
Prof. Anurag Gupta
Head, Department of Mechanical Engineering, IIT Kanpur  |  head_me@iitk.ac.in for any further details.
CONTRIBUTE TO ME
Your contribution directly benefits students, faculty, and research at IIT Kanpur's Department of Mechanical Engineering.

=== givingback opp school support cbse affiliation and student scholarships ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/opp-school-support-CBSE-affiliation-and-student-scholarships.php
 Opportunity School - Support CBSE affiliation and Student Scholarships
  0 Contributors
0%
0 Contributors raised Rs. 0 of Goal Amount Rs. 20,00,000
Give Back Now
To the Class of 1970
Your generous contribution of ₹18.6 lakhs in the Academic Year 2025–26 towards the Opportunity School at IIT Kanpur has played a vital role in upgrading key facilities and enhancing the learning environment for all students. Detailed reports on the progress have been shared with you.
For many children at Opportunity School, education stops at Class 8- not by choice, but by circumstance.
At present, the school supports students up to Class 8, offering them a strong academic foundation and a nurturing environment. However, beyond Class 8, many students face a critical challenge. While some are compelled to discontinue their education, others who aspire to continue must transition to schools with significantly higher fees up to ₹3000 per month, compared to ₹400 per month at Opportunity School placing a considerable financial burden on their families.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback opp school support cbse affiliation and student s]
Our Vision is to ensure that every child is able to complete at least Class 10, enabling access to better opportunities through higher education or vocational pathways.
To make this possible, we have initiated:
CBSE affiliation for Classes 9 and 10.
Upgradation and repair of school infrastructure.
The Phase II school building, a major milestone, has already been made possible through alumni support and will soon be operational.
Where your support is needed:
CBSE Affiliation: One-time requirement of ₹1,50,000 for application, inspection, and formalities
Scholarships for Students
Scholarships will support students with tuition fees, books, uniforms, as well as laboratory and examination expenses. A corpus of ₹10 lakhs can generate approximately ₹40,000 per year, supporting two students with ₹20,000 each or four students with ₹10,000 each. With a planned intake of 30 students per class, expanding this support is essential.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback opp school support cbse affiliation and student s]
Other requirements include the addition of teaching staff and strengthening key facilities such as a mathematics laboratory, upgraded science laboratory, enhanced library, basic sports infrastructure, and infirmary facilities. (These are expected to be supported through institutional and alumni contributions.)
Be a Part of This Journey
We seek your support specifically towards CBSE affiliation and scholarship funding.
Your contribution will directly enable students to continue their education beyond Class 8 and complete their schooling with dignity and opportunity.

=== givingback opportunity school ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/opportunity-school
 Opportunity School
  371 Contributors
Give Back Now
Opportunity School is a 52 year old school providing education to the underprivileged children living in and around IIT Kanpur campus. It came into existence because the children of people working as domestic help had no hope of getting education. It has progressed from 6 students and a Type-2 house to about 300 students and a block in Kendriya Vidyalaya.
Education to the underprivileged children is the only way to brighten their future through self-empowerment. And it is also the best way to eradicate child labour from society. The school has been functioning all these years by adhering to the principles of Sarva Shiksha Abhiyan. We try to help our children acquire skills, habits, and attitude to adjust well in the society.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback opportunity school]
At present the school runs classes from 1 to 8 and is recognized by the UP Board of Education. We are trying to go up to class 10 as many children, especially girls, drop out of school after class 8 because their parents are not prepared to send them to expensive schools outside the campus.
We have also started identifying individual talent and providing opportunity to hone them by inviting specialized teachers for dance, art, jewellery making, quilling, mehendi application.
Our students have also started acquiring computer skills, using an interactive science lab, and learning book craft and home science.
Some Glance Pic of Opportunity School

=== givingback p k kelkar library ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/p-k-kelkar-library
Maintenance & Upgradation of P K Kelkar Library
4%
Rs. 8,34,508
Contributed	Rs. 2,10,00,000
Goal Amount
11 Contributors
Give Back Now
Brief details of proposed up gradation works in PK Kelkar Library Building.
Background
The Library building was constructed in year 1963.
The building is basement + three storied with ground coverage of 2410 sq. meter & plinth area 5000 sq. meter.
Existing condition of building
The building structure condition is satisfactory.
The false ceiling and vinyl flooring requires up gradation. The false ceiling was done in 1996-97.
Internal painting is required.
Need of Upgradation
The false ceiling needs to be changed and as well as to be painted internally on effected area.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback p k kelkar library]
The vinyl flooring in common and circulation areas of all floors needs to be changed.
Proposed Upgradation Works
Dismantling of old false ceiling in all three floors.
New false ceiling with under deck insulation.
Removal of old vinyl flooring and P/Laying 2 mm thick new Vinyl floor in all basement + three floors.
Repairing and distempering of effected walls.
Painting on windows.
Some aluminium glass partitions with door.
Rates
The up gradation cost of civil work is based on the DSR-2018.
Total proposed upgradation Cost
The total up gradation cost as per abstract of cost shall come to Rs. 1,51,84,000.00

=== givingback prayas ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/prayas
 Prayas
  154 Contributors
Give Back Now
 In short, the Aim of PRAYAS is 'to Inspire and Enable'.
PRAYAS is an endeavour of IITK students, mainly focussed on providing education to the marginalized kids in and around IITK campus, atleast upto the level where they too can dream big.Presently a club under the Presidential Council in the Students' Gymkhana, IIT Kanpur.Its a voluntary service to promote the notion of 'Education for All' as a sustainable means of development.
AIM
To make quality education accessible to those who are deprived of it.
To inculcate values and provide moral education to the students.
To promote personal development of the students through various co-curricular activities like Music, Sports, Painting, Debates, Dance, Poetry, etc.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback prayas]
To create awareness about Health and Social issues and also about the 'Need of Education' among the students.
To develop a methodology of teaching that would attract the kids towards Education and prevent Drop-outs.

=== givingback previous campaigns ===

Prof. Kanwar Singh Nalwa Memorial Scholarship

=== givingback prof vk singh distinguished lecture series ===

About Prof. Vinod K. Singh
Professor Vinod K. Singh is a distinguished academician, scientist, and leader in the field of Synthetic Organic Chemistry, with a career spanning over three decades. Currently, he is an emeritus professor at IITK holding the Rahul and Namita Gautam Chair. He is also holding the position of Director's Chair Professor at IISER Bhopal, Professor Singh has made groundbreaking contributions to both research and the management of higher educational institutions. His leadership extends beyond academia, with key roles in governing scientific research councils, national educational policies, and mentoring future generations of scientists. Recognized globally for his expertise, Professor Singh has been honored with prestigious awards like the Padma Shri (2014), Shanti Swarup Bhatnagar Prize (2004), Vigyan Ratna Award of U.P. (2006), J.C. Bose fellowship (2009), Samman Patra by UP Government (2018) reflecting his profound impact on science and education in India and beyond.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback prof vk singh distinguished lecture series]

Distinguished Lecture Series - Department of Chemistry
The Distinguished Lecture Series aims to feature engaging speakers who present various perspectives on organic chemistry in an accessible and captivating manner. The lecture series is designed for a broad audience, encouraging participation from across the IIT Kanpur community, including:
Students
Faculty members
Staff members
Research scholars
Eminent Speakers
The lecture series invites thought leaders and visionaries from a wide range of fields, such as:
Leading scientists and researchers
Technologists and innovators
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback prof vk singh distinguished lecture series]
Entrepreneurs and change-makers
Government and academic professionals
These distinguished speakers share their insights on life, education, career, and research, inspiring and engaging the IIT Kanpur fraternity. Each session fosters meaningful discussions and showcases the diverse expertise of the speakers.
The objective of Professor Vinod K Singh’s Lecture Series is:
To biennially honor an eminent Scientist/Faculty of a prestigious research and/or academic institute of India, who excels in teaching and research, by inviting them to deliver a lecture to the Institute’s academic community.
Extension of the invitation to international speakers in the near future is also envisaged.
The lecture series is expected to cater to a wide range of researchers and graduate students in terms of exposure to the broad understanding of the scope of theoretical and computational chemistry, and for encouraging cutting-edge research in this area.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback prof vk singh distinguished lecture series]

=== givingback scholarship ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/scholarship
 Student Scholarship
On Going Scholarship
Donate For Student Welfare Activities
Partial and full student scholarship support and merit awards are provided to students who are not eligible for government scholarships. Scholarships are mainly provided to UG students as the PG programmes are fully covered by the government. Around 10% of the UG students are also covered under the social welfare programs of the government.
Impact
The student scholarships at IIT Kanpur are aimed at enabling needy and meritorious students to excel in their academic endeavors and turn their aspirations into a reality. The awards recognize merit and provide better opportunities to students both in terms of higher education and career opportunities.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback scholarship]
Criticality
The current fund position allows us to support only 10 to 25% of the total expenditure of the student for a year including tuition fees, living costs and other miscellaneous expenditure given the increase in tuition fees (even though it is still highly subsidized). The number of scholarships available provides benefits to not more than 15% of the yearly applicants.
Click here to know more about tuition fees.

Above 5 lakhs
Between 1 and 5 lakhs
Less than 1 lakh

=== givingback scholarship 67 ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/scholarship-67.php
Scholarship-67
Give Back Now
As alumni of IIT Kanpur, we cherish the bonds forged during our time together and seek to perpetuate the spirit of camaraderie and excellence that defined our era. In tribute to those who have left an indelible mark on our hearts and minds, we would like to honour the memory of our departed friends by establishing the perpetual ‘Scholarship-67’ by the batchmates who graduated in 1967. This initiative serves as a testament to the enduring legacy of our cohort, offering a beacon of hope and opportunity to deserving students who embody the values and ideals we hold dear.
Through the generosity and support of our fellow alumni, we aim to create a lasting impact by providing financial assistance to deserving students at IITK as they pursue their educational endeavors. By investing in the academic success of future generations, we ensure that the legacy of the Class of 1967 continues to thrive and inspire for all years to come.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback scholarship 67]

=== givingback shiksha sopan ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/shiksha-sopan
 Shiksha Sopan
  58 Contributors
Give Back Now
Shiksha Sopan is a non-governmental organization (NGO) whose purpose is to impart value- based education to economically weaker section of the society. Its various activities are spreaded in and around the lush-green campus of IIT Kanpur. Shiksha Sopan's philosophy stands on three pillars of Shiksha, Sanskar, and Swavalamban.
We believe that education is not merely loading children with information. Therefore, we try our best to establish a connection of class-room teaching with daily-life activities and behaviors. With this point in mind, we keep organizing various activities that are aimed to further nurture these values.

=== givingback swami vivekananda vidyalaya ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/swami-vivekananda-vidyalaya
 Swami Vivekananda Vidyalaya
  17 Contributors
Give Back Now
Swami Vivekananda Vidyalaya is a major centre of Jagriti Bal Vikas Samiti, Kanpur.This school was founded at lodhar village in 1996. Those days education level was very low in this village. Near about 90% children were deprived from school life . although there was a primary school in the village but children did not like to go. Children used to graze animals whole day and guardians economic condition was too worse to maintain their livelihood .
In this condition it was only a dream to provide education in a better school for guardians . to convert their dreams in realty Swami Vivekanand Vidyalaya was founded .it was tried to bring them in the focus of society by providing them interesting education .Due to Endeavor of teachers’ and volunteers children began to take interest in studies and guardians attitude changed they considered for awareness . today 100% girls and boys of lodhar are getting education . being impressed by teaching – system of this school students are coming on foot from remote village which are about 5 to 6 km distance .
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback swami vivekananda vidyalaya]

=== givingback young faculty fellowship ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/givingback/young-faculty-fellowship
Young faculty Research Fellowships
Young faculty Research Fellowships have been created by the institute for those young Faculty members who are below the age of 40 years. Similar to Faculty Chair, Research Faculty Fellowship is conferred upon a Young faculty member for a period of three years which may be renewed based on the performance and age criteria. Faculty Research Fellowships are set up with the generous support in the form of donation from our alumni and well-wishers. The Fellowship may be named after the donor or name of the Batch.
There is a need to increase the numbers to honour more outstanding young faculty members in the institute.Faculty Fellowships are awarded for a period of three years under two categories Young Faculty Research Fellowships to those who are below the age of 40 years and have made outstanding contribution in their research areas.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > givingback young faculty fellowship]
Endowment required Rs. 45,00,000 (for paying a monthly honorarium of Rs. 15,000) New Faculty Fellowships to the newly joined young faculty members as special incentives for research and development, setting up laboratories and experimental facilities, buying expensive books, etc. Endowment required Rs. 75,00,000 (for paying a monthly honorarium of Rs. 25,000)

=== gym expansion upgrade ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/gym-expansion-upgrade
Gym Upgrade & Expansion
Gym Upgrade & Expansion
IIT Kanpur’s Old Sports Complex gymnasium recently underwent a major transformation, supported by the generous contribution of the Class of 1970. The upgraded facility, whose foundation stone was laid in February 2023 and inaugurated in August 2024, stands as a reflection of the Institute’s commitment to promoting health and wellness among its community members.The new gym has significantly improved the campus’s sports and fitness infrastructure, reflecting the growing enthusiasm for physical well-being among students and staff.
The renovated gym now includes modern fitness machines and larger workout areas to meet the growing needs of the campus community. It has quickly become a vibrant space for students to pursue their fitness goals, fostering an environment that encourages both personal discipline and collective motivation.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > gym expansion upgrade]
The Institute expresses its sincere gratitude to the Class of 1970 for their thoughtful contribution and continued engagement with IIT Kanpur. Special thanks go to Mr. Rajiv Sahay, Mr. Surojit Sen, and Mr. Ashok Singh for leading this initiative. Their efforts exemplify the spirit of giving back and the lasting bond between IIT Kanpur and its alumni community.

HITKARINI TIWARI
TECHNOLOGY X
CHALLENGE
(HT2X)
CHALLENGE
Prof. Sandip Tiwari
Prof. Sandip Tiwari is an Indian-born electrical engineer and applied physicist renowned for his pioneering contributions to nanotechnology, semiconductor devices and quantum- effect technologies. Prof.Tiwari, an alumnus of IIT Kanpur (B.Tech., Electrical Engineering), was honored with the institute's Distinguished Alumnus Award (DAA) in 2003 for his outstanding contributions to science and engineering. After his time at IIT Kanpur, he pursued his Masters and Ph.D. and went on to become the Charles N. Mellowes Professor in Engineering with affiliation in Electrical and Computer Engineering and Applied Physics at Cornell University, while also serving as the Director of the academic national nanotechnology network efforts of USA. Before transitioning to academia full time, he played a significant role in advancing technology at IBM Research. A Fellow of the IEEE and APS, he has made lasting contributions to nanoelectronics and quantum technologies that have been recognized by numerous international awards. He is also the author of several key textbooks as an Electroscience series published by Oxford University Press. Read More
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > hitkarini]
Challenge
Develop creative solutions that address critical problem statements
Problem Statement-1
[Amphibian robot]: UAUV-Unmanned Aerial Underwater Vehicle
Design and deploy a fully autonomous aerial and then submersible vehicle capable of detecting, safely acquiring predefined object inside the water, to its initial launch site. The system must execute this mission without human intervention, relying entirely on onboard processing, computer vision, autonomous navigation, and robotic manipulation to secure the payload under adverse aerial and aquatic conditions. View More
Problem Statement-2
[Two body robotic interaction]: Symbiotic Aerial-Ground or Aerial-Aerial Autonomous System (SAGAS or SAAAS)
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > hitkarini]
Design a cooperative system consisting of a terrestrial rover and an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV). Teams must design these two distinct platforms to function interdependently in a complex, obstacle-rich environment. The system must overcome the inherent limitations of each individual platform—specifically, the limited line-of-sight of the rover and the restricted battery life of the drone—by establishing a symbiotic relationship featuring collaborative mapping and dynamic, mobile (drone on ground vehicle) docking. View More
Challenge Rules
1
Five teams(Students from IIT Kanpur) will be selected by the committe based on the proposals submitted.Each team will comprise of at least one female member,a faculty advisor, team lead, deputy team lead and team members.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > hitkarini]
2
The duration of the project is one year; however, extensions for certain teams may be granted based on performance and subject to the committee's recommendation.
3
Selected teams will receive up to ₹1.5 lakh each to execute their project.
4
Mid-term and final evaluations will be conducted to monitor the progress of the teams and additional financial incentives will be awarded to top performing teams based on the evaluation (₹40,000 per team).
Proposal Format
Title of the project
Introduction
Deliverables :
(Mention any prototype that will be developed)
Budget and Timeline
Team Members:
Details of the team members (email, contact number)
Deadlines
Proposal Submission: 05 Sep 2026,
Selection of proposals and Result announcement : to be updated soon
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > hitkarini]

Frequently Asked Questions
Quick answers to common queries about the HT2X Challenge
Q:
Who owns the intellectual property (IP) for the project?
IP remains with the institute.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > hitkarini]
Q:
What is the budget allotted for the challenge?
₹1.5 lakh per team.
Q:
What should teams include in their budget?
Include a clear budget breakdown and justification in the proposal.
Q:
Who are eligible to register in the challenge?
Only enrolled IIT Kanpur students.
Q:
Is there a maximum team size?
No specific limit, but a female member is mandatory.
Q:
Is there a registration fee?
No, there are no registration charges.
Q:
Can students use institute facilities like Makerspace?
Yes, students can use Makerspace facilities for their projects.
Q:

dora_desk@iitk.ac.in
Q:
How many teams will be selected, and what do they receive?
Five teams will be selected to receive full budget allocation for project execution.

Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/IITKDF.php
IITK Development Foundation
IIT Kanpur is a Government of India Funded Technical Institute. Established in 1960, its mission is to enable a creative and dynamic learning environment, build industry-academic collaboration, and develop sustainable research solutions for the benefit of the nation. The Institute is dependent on Central Government funding to the extent of over 75% of its needs. As IITK grows, the gap between Central Government funds and the Institute needs is widening. With the ever-growing number of departments, faculty and students, there is a constant need to raise funds for the various programs and initiatives run by the Institute. To innovate cutting-edge technology, state-of-the-art R&D facilities need to be build; our faculty are known for their groundbreaking research, it is thus imperative to provide fellowships that will motivate and encourage them to produce path-breaking research; we run many scholarship programs that give financial aid, especially to economically weak students and motivate girl students to continue their studies. IIT Kanpur believes in inclusivity and thus runs many community welfare programs as well, such as Opportunity School, Shiksha Sopan, Prayaas that provide education and vocational training to children from the neighboring villages of the Campus.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > iitkdf]
Hence, with the ever growing need to raise funds for the various activities/programs of the Institute, a separate professional fund raising organization for IIT Kanpur was created, IIT Kanpur Development Foundation. It is a section-8 company of the Institute approved by the Board of Governors in June 2020.
Chief Executive Officer
Mr. Kapil Kaul
CEO IIT Kanpur Development Foundation
   kapilkaul@iitk.ac.in
  +91 512-6796979
Mr. Kapil is a seasoned fundraising professional with close to 20 years of proven leadership and rich experience of working with diverse stakeholders and organisations across; Higher Education, Non-Profit and Banking sectors. He is skilled in creating and implementing fundraising strategies, building organisations from scratch, building relationships, business development, securing high value philanthropic gifts, CSR, mass market fundraising, people development, stakeholder management, sales, and P&L management.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > iitkdf]
Mr. Kaul has rich, multi-sectoral experience of working with some of the leading brands across the domains of banking, non-profit and higher education. His professional journey started with HDFC Bank where he joined as a part of the first batch of management trainees. He went on to work with brands like ABN AMRO, Deutsche Bank, Royal Bank of Scotland, Save the Children, and Indian Institute for Human Settlements. His last professional assignment was with IIT Bombay where he was leading the Development Office of the Institute.
Mr. Kaul is a management graduate from University of Mumbai and completed his undergraduate degree in commerce from Hindu College, Delhi University.

=== kislaya ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/kislaya
KISLAYA NURSERY SCHOOL
Kislaya Nursery School, established in 1967 and situated within the IIT Kanpur campus, continues to set benchmarks in pre-primary education, guided by the philosophy of Joyful Learning. The academic session 2024-25 witnessed a steady increase in student strength, which led to the expansion of the teaching staff from 6 to 8 members. This growth reflects the school’s ongoing commitment to providing individualized attention and nurturing a culturally rich, developmentally appropriate environment for every child.
Infrastructure & Academics
During the academic year 2024–25, Kislaya Nursery School undertook several infrastructure improvements to enhance comfort and organization. These included the installation of 9 exhaust fans and 13 wall-mounted fans in classrooms to manage summer heat, construction of 2 new storerooms for efficient supply management, renovation of the school office with new tiling, and the addition of a stepwise staircase for the stage based on parent feedback to support student performances.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kislaya]
Academically, the school operated 3 Nursery sections, 2 KG sections, and 2 Prep sections, with a total student strength of 186. All admissions and fee transactions were carried out through cashless systems, ensuring transparency and ease. The dedicated team of eight teachers continued to provide a nurturing environment that combined emotional support, foundational learning, and cultural values, making education a joyful and enriching experience for every child.
School Activities
Throughout the academic year 2024-25, Kislaya Nursery School organized a vibrant array of hands-on activities designed to nurture creativity, imagination, and essential life skills in our young learners.These activities encouraged exploration, teamwork, and sensory development, making learning both meaningful and joyous. These included collage work, freehand drawing and colouring, origami, clay modelling, diya and candle decoration, and various forms of printing arts such as thumb, vegetable, cotton, and bud printing. Pasting activities using matchsticks, glitter, buttons, paper, and bindis enhanced fine motor skills and attention to detail. Celebratory crafts like Mother’s Day card making and rakhi making nurtured emotional expression and cultural awareness. Themed sessions such as the transport activity and storytelling with props enriched conceptual understanding and imagination. Regular poem recitation boosted confidence and language development, while simple science experiments ignited curiosity and a love for discovery. Educational excursions like the zoo trip to Allen Forest Zoo in Kanpur and the Children’s Day picnic at Park No. 67 offered experiential learning and social interaction. Festive events such as the Christmas Bounce House Day brought joy and added to the vibrant learning environment.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kislaya]
Sports
Sports and physical activities are an essential part of life at Kislaya Nursery School, with every Thursday dedicated to Sports and PT sessions under the guidance of Coach Jay Tripathi. The much-awaited Annual Sports Day, held in November 2024, was a grand celebration of spirit and healthy competition, where every child participated with enthusiasm and joy. Highlights of the year included the March-Past, Mass PT, aerobics, taekwondo, the ever-popular KG football match, and a variety of competitive races such as the balloon race, hurdle race, and even a tug of war for parents. Through these activities, children not only developed coordination, endurance, and physical confidence but also learned the values of discipline, sportsmanship, and teamwork making sports at Kislaya a foundation for healthy habits and joyful learning.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kislaya]
Celebrations
Celebrating festivals builds cultural awareness and emotional connections. Kislaya believes in experiential learning through stories, costumes, dance, and food.
Independence Day: Flag hoisting, National Anthem, student performances
Teacher's Day: Students dressed as their class teachers and shared cards
Raksha Bandhan: Rakhi making, tying ceremony, and dances
Janmashtami: Jhanki decoration, Krishna stories, and dance
Dussehra: Storytelling, Dandiya party, and Ram-Leela
Diwali: Diya making, paper lanterns, puja, rangoli, firework show
Christmas: Santa-themed day with bounce house play and gift exchange
Republic Day: Flag hoisting, performances, and speeches on freedom fighters These moments strengthen students’ values, their understanding of tradition, and create lifelong memories.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kislaya]
Class Parties
Kislaya believes in learning through joyful experiences, where celebration becomes learning in disguise. This year, the school organized the Lemonade Party, where children mixed and served lemonade, learning teamwork and responsibility. The Mango Party introduced students to different mango varieties while helping them understand fruits and seasonal changes. These fun-filled activities supported the development of motor skills, food awareness, and sharing habits, while fostering joyful peer interactions.
Annual Function
The Annual Function was held on 1st March 2025 at IIT Kanpur’s Main Auditorium, with 100% student participation—a proud Kislaya tradition. Dance teachers Gautam Srivastava and Rohith Shah choreographed a range of lively performances. Highlights included Saraswati Vandana, a play on Subhash Chandra Bose, salsa, folk dances, and theme-based acts on Krishna and environmental awareness. The event concluded with prize distribution and refreshments for the students. The enthusiasm, joy, and appreciation from parents made it a truly memorable celebration.

=== kotak school ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/kotak-school
Kotak School of Sustainability
About the Initiative
In a landmark initiative supported through Kotak Mahindra Bank’s CSR program, IIT Kanpur established the Kotak School of Sustainability—India’s first fully integrated and interdisciplinary school dedicated to sustainability. The school aims to address global and national climate challenges through advanced research, education, and outreach.
Prof. Sachchida Nand Tripathi is the inaugural Dean of Kotak School of Sustainability at IIT Kanpur and a Professor in the Department of Civil Engineering and the Department of Sustainable Energy Engineering.
Academic Integration
Approved New M. Tech Program on “Artificial Intelligence for Sustainability” under the school: A key milestone is the formal approval and rollout of a new interdisciplinary MTech program in “Artificial Intelligence for Sustainability,” designed to equip students with technical expertise and contextual understanding to apply AI in solving pressing sustainability challenges.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kotak school]
In addition, the School offers a PhD program in Artificial Intelligence for Sustainability, the highest academic degree awarded by the School. This program is ideal for individuals aiming for careers in academia, industry, or government and focuses on a range of key areas, including Climate Change, Resilience and Adaptation, Modeling, Water and Water Resource Engineering, Sustainable Cities, and the intersection of Artificial Intelligence and Sustainability.
Collaborations: HESCO, Dehradun; Kanpur Smart City Ltd.; INSA, New Delhi; Nanyang Technological University, Singapore. Research & Innovation and Outreach Events are as specified in the document.
Collaborations
To further its sustainability mission, the School has established partnerships with several leading institutions:
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kotak school]
HESCO, Dehradun: Joint efforts on the development of a Sustainability Index and outreach initiatives.
Kanpur Smart City Ltd. : Collaboration on technology development aimed at achieving Net Zero Waste.
INSA, New Delhi: Partnership focused on leveraging IIT Kanpur and INSA’s strengths for advancing science, technology, and innovation (STI) policy.
Nanyang Technological University, Singapore: Collaborative research on novel membrane technologies for water and wastewater treatment.
Research & Innovation
Research efforts under the School include the development of field-deployable, scalable technologies, with pilot testing of low-cost air pollution sensors and membrane filtration units currently underway in Kanpur and Vidisha. As part of Net Zero initiatives, a campus-wide energy audit has been completed, and the implementation of solar PV systems and waste segregation units has been initiated.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > kotak school]
OUTREACH EVENTS
National Conference on Innovative AI Solutions for Sustainable Cities 2025 was held on February 3, 2025
IIT Kanpur Swastibhavatu Lecture Series was held on March 25th, 2025. The inaugural lecture was delivered by Padma Shri Prof. Thalappil Pradeep (IIT Madras)
Workshop on ‘Watershed Development under Pradhan Mantri Krishi Sinchayee Yojana (PMKSY) was held on April 4th 2025.
A series of KSS Seminars were held, featuring distinguished speakers including Prof. Raghu Murtugudde, Dr. Mukund Rao, Dr. Anil Prakash Joshi, and Prof. Anu Ramaswami.

=== new initiatives departmentalfund ashokesen ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/departmentalFund/ashokeSen.php
Prof. Ashoke Sen Endowment
Prof. Ashoke Sen is a renowned Indian theoretical physicist and currently serves as a faculty member and ICTS-Infosys Madhava Chair Professor at the International Centre for Theoretical Sciences, Tata Institute of Fundamental Research. He is known for his pioneering contributions to string theory, including S-duality and tachyon condensation. He is a recipient of the Fundamental Physics Prize and the Padma Bhushan, among several other prestigious awards.
Prof. Sen has generously contributed to the modernization of the Seminar Room in the Department of Physics, dedicating it in honor of Prof. Amal Kumar Raychaudhuri, a distinguished Indian theoretical physicist. Prof. Raychaudhuri made significant contributions to General Relativity and, during his tenure at Presidency College, Calcutta University, mentored and inspired generations of eminent Indian physicists, including Prof. Sen.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund ashokesen]
The Ashok Sen Endowment Fund continues to play a vital role in upgrading and maintenance of the Prof. Anmol Kumar Raychaudhuri Seminar Hall at the Department of Physics. In the academic year 2024-25, the fund was utilized to upgrade the Seminar Hall, which remains a central venue for seminars (both in physical and hybrid modes), colloquiums, group discussions, and various departmental activities.
Significant improvements were made during this period, including the installation of a comprehensive audio-visual system to support modern presentation and conferencing needs and recording the lectures. Additionally, the chairs in the seminar hall were repaired and dry-cleaned, improving both functionality and aesthetics. These upgrades have greatly enhanced the usability and comfort of the facility, and the fund has proven invaluable in facilitating these much-needed improvements.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund ashokesen]
“We are deeply grateful for this fund that gives us dedicated support for continuous dissemination of Physics ideas across the whole community.”
- Department of Physics

=== new initiatives departmentalfund chemicalengineering ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/departmentalFund/chemicalEngineering.php
Chemical Engineering Foundation
The Chemical Engineering Foundation includes the Jeet Bindra Student Travel Grant and the Jeet Bindra Distinguished Lecture Series, both established by Mr. Jagjeet Singh Bindra (BT/CHE/1969) in the Department of Chemical Engineering at IIT Kanpur.
Jeet Bindra Student Travel Grant
The Jeet Bindra Student Travel Grant aims to facilitate the academic and professional growth of students in Chemical Engineering by enabling them to present their research at international conferences. This grant not only provides financial assistance but also encourages students to engage with the global academic community, enhancing their research experience and broadening their professional networks. Through this endowment, Mr. Bindra continues to contribute to the advancement of engineering education and research at IIT Kanpur.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund chemicalengineering]
For FY 2024-25 Jeet Bindra Student Travel Grant was awarded to Ms. Sajal Wankhede and Mr. A . S. RUSSEL
Sajal Wankhede at PRiME 2024 Conference, Honolulu
Sajal Wankhede presented his research, “Nature of Instability in Electrokinetics-based Anodic Oxide Pore Formation,” at the Pacific Rim Meeting on Electrochemical and Solid-State Science (PRiME 2024) held in Honolulu, Hawaii.
I would like to express my heartfelt gratitude to Mr. Jeet Bindra for his generous support through the Jeet Bindra Travel Grant, which enabled my participation in the Pacific Rim Meeting on Electrochemical and Solid-State Science (PRiME 2024) in Honolulu, Hawaii.
“SAJAL WANKHEDE”
A. S. Russel at EuChemS Chemistry Congress, Ireland
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund chemicalengineering]
A. S. Russel presented a paper titled “Insights into Promotional Effect of Cobalt in Ni-Co Catalysts through DFT-Aided Microkinetic Modelling during Oxidative Dry Reforming of Methane” at the EuChemS Chemistry Congress held in Ireland.
I sincerely thank Mr. Jeet Bindra for the financial support that enabled me to attend the 9th EuChemS Chemistry Congress in Dublin. This opportunity allowed me to present my research to a global audience, engage with pioneers in the field, and explore collaborations and receive career opportunities as a postdoctoral researcher.
“A. S. RUSSEL”
Jeet Bindra Distinguished Lecture Series
Mr. Jagjeet Singh Bindra, a distinguished alumnus, has instituted the Jeet Bindra Distinguished Lecture Series in the Department of Chemical Engineering at IIT Kanpur. The series invites eminent scientists and industry leaders from India and abroad each year to deliver insightful lectures, promoting knowledge exchange, networking, and insights into academia, research, and careers.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund chemicalengineering]
Jeet Bindra Distinguished Lecture 2025
Speaker: Prof. Kamal Kishore Pant
Prof. Pant spoke on the "Redefining the Future of Energy: Sustainable Pathways and Technological Innovations”.
An IIT Kanpur alumnus with a Ph.D. in Chemical Engineering, Prof. Pant is currently the Director of IIT Roorkee. His research focuses on heterogeneous catalysis, clean energy, sustainable resource recovery, and circular economy technologies, with an emphasis on carbon-efficient solutions for India's energy and environmental challenges.
Jeet Bindra Distinguished Lecture 2024
Speaker : Dr. Ramesh Gupta
Dr. Gupta spoke on “The Carbon Challenge: Background, Current Status and Evolving Carbon Capture Technologies”.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund chemicalengineering]
Dr. Ramesh Gupta, an IIT Kanpur alumnus (BT/CHE/1969), earned his Ph.D. from Princeton University and began his career at Caltech. He joined Exxon (now ExxonMobil) in 1977, where he spent over four decades working on reactor engineering, refining processes, carbon capture, and process modeling, while also serving as an adjunct professor at Washington University in St. Louis. A recipient of multiple Exxon awards and the holder of 50+ U.S. patents, he continues to contribute as a technical consultant from New Jersey.

Through this initiative, Mr. Rao has strengthened research exposure and academic excellence within the department.The initiative helps researchers present their work, gain feedback, build global networks, and stay updated with emerging trends.

=== new initiatives departmentalfund researchifoundation ===

Research I Foundation
The Research-I Foundation, established in 2003 by Shri N. R. Narayana Murthy, Founder of Infosys Limited and a distinguished alumnus of IIT Kanpur, has been a cornerstone of the Department of Computer Science and Engineering’s research ecosystem. As a generous benefactor, his sustained support over more than two decades has significantly strengthened the department’s research output and fostered a dynamic, high-impact research environment for both faculty and students. The Foundation provides vital assistance for presenting research at international conferences, supports young faculty in advancing their research, enables B.Tech students to participate in prestigious platforms such as the ACM ICPC World Finals, and facilitates invited talks by leading researchers, among other initiatives.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund researchifoundation]
Activities conducted during the FY 2024- 25
Lecture series
The Foundation conducted lectures under the “Eyes-on-Research” lecture series to promote research.
The talks included “Robust Query Processing: Where Geometry Beats” by Professor Jayant R. Haritsa (IISc, Bangalore).
“The Side Benefits of Knowing an Algorithm” by Professor Jaikumar Radhakrishnan (ICTS, Bangalore).
As part of the invited talk series, the department hosted distinguished speakers from leading international institutions.
Professor Andreas Herzig (CNRS, IRIT, France) delivered a talk titled “Reasoning About Trust”.
Professor Margaret Burnett (Oregon State University, USA) presented “To Enable Diverse Mere Mortals to Assess an AI Agent's Goodness for Their Own Needs”.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives departmentalfund researchifoundation]

Prof. Angshuman Karmakar, visited COSIC, KU Leuven, Belgium, and presented a paper at the 22nd International Conference on Applied Cryptography and Network Security (ACNS 2024) in Abu Dhabi, UAE.
Prof. Indranil Saha, visited Hong Kong to deliver an invited talk and present a paper at the International Conference on Cyber-Physical Systems (ICCPS 2024).
Prof. Subhajit Roy visited Luxembourg to present a paper at TACAS 2024 and attend a Dagstuhl seminar.
Prof. Urbi Chatterjee, visited Hamburg, Germany to attend Embedded Systems Week (ESWEEK) and participated in the UC Santa Cruz-IIT Delhi-IIT Kanpur Joint Symposium on Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, and Cybersecurity.

ICPC World Finals
The International Collegiate Programming Contest (ICPC) is the oldest and one of the most respected global programming competitions, bringing together top student programmers from around the world. It challenges participants to showcase their algorithmic skills, problem-solving capabilities, and teamwork. A team from IIT Kanpur represented the institute at the ICPC World Finals 2023 in Luxor, Egypt (April 2024) and the ICPC World Finals 2024 in Astana, Kazakhstan after qualifying through the ICPC Asia West regional contests. Participating in the World Finals provided the team an opportunity to compete with and interact with top programmers from leading universities around the world.

Arepalli Travel Grant
In memory of his parents, Dr. Sivaram Arepalli instituted the Arepalli-Karumuri Family Grant in the Department of Physics in 2018. It supports one annual travel grant for a student of Physics to attend and deliver an invited talk at an international conference organized by reputed scientific societies such as APS, ACS, or MRS. In addition, the recipient must have a paper published or accepted in leading journals such as Nature, Science, PNAS, or PRL.
During FY 2024-25, three students were supported to present their research at prestigious international platforms:
Ruchika Dhaka delivered a talk titled “Exploring the Broadband Spectral and Timing Characteristics of GRS 1915+105 with AstroSat and NICER” at the MAXI 15 Years Conference for Time-Domain Astronomy held at Nihon University, Tokyo, Japan.

Priyanka Meena attended the APS Global Physics Summit 2025 organized by the American Physical Society in Anaheim, California, USA.

=== new initiatives others cdap ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/others/cdap.php
OTHER INITIATIVES
CDAP - Cell for Differently Abled Persons
The Cell for Differently Abled Persons (CDAP) at IIT Kanpur is committed to fostering an inclusive, accessible, and equitable campus environment for students with disabilities. Established to ensure equal opportunities in academics, campus life, and personal development, CDAP works closely with students, faculty, and various institute units to provide reasonable accommodations, accessibility support, assistive services, and sensitization programmes.
A significant milestone in CDAP's journey has been the implementation of the Student Disability Program, generously supported by Mr. Ranodeb Roy, a distinguished alumnus of IIT Kanpur. This initiative has enabled the Cell to strengthen disability support services through enhanced accessibility measures, procurement of assistive technologies, development of inclusive infrastructure, awareness sessions, and student-centric interventions that improve academic participation and campus life.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others cdap]
During the financial year 2024-2025, CDAP undertook targeted interventions to support students with different disabilities by providing customized assistive solutions.
Electric Wheelchair Support
A postgraduate student with locomotor disability was provided with an electric wheelchair, enabling her to move independently across campus. This intervention significantly improved mobility, reduced dependence on external assistance, and enhanced participation in academic activities.
KIBO Assistive Reading Device
An assistive reading device (KIBO 360) was installed in the library for visually impaired students. The device converts printed and digital text into audio, allowing students to independently access academic resources and study materials.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others cdap]
The initiatives undertaken by CDAP have led to significant improvements in the lives of differently abled students. The provision of assistive devices has enhanced mobility and independence for locomotor-disabled students, while tools like assistive reading technologies have improved academic accessibility for visually impaired students. The program has ensured effective utilization of funds by focusing on need-based solutions tailored to individual requirements. These efforts have collectively strengthened the inclusive learning environment at IIT Kanpur and empowered students to pursue their education with greater confidence, independence, and dignity.

=== new initiatives others healthtech ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/others/healthTech.php
OTHER INITIATIVES
Health Tech Ideation & Innovation Grant (HII Program)
Instituted by Mr. Ajay Dubey (BT/CHE/1980) and his wife, Mrs. Rooma Dubey, the HII Program aims to seed and nurture innovations in health technology while building an ecosystem that supports student startups in the HealthTech domain. The program exposes students to real-world problem statements, encouraging them to develop innovative technological solutions for the betterment of the healthcare system.
Under the umbrella of SIIC, IIT Kanpur, students receive funding, mentorship, and networking opportunities to accelerate their growth and foster a culture of entrepreneurship. For more information, please visit E-Cell, IIT Kanpur.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others healthtech]
Mr. Ajay Dubey holds a bachelor's degree in Chemical Engineering from IIT Kanpur and has over 40 years of experience in the IT industry. He has served as Vice President at Infosys and headed the Infosys Pune Development Center, where he was responsible for establishing the Pune operations. He has also served as the Chief Operating Officer at Persistent Systems Pvt. Ltd. and is an angel investor in Clodura.ai. He is an avid reader and a keen follower of modern technology trends.
The Healthcare Innovation & Ideation (HII) Program made notable progress in fostering health-tech innovation among IIT Kanpur students during the 2024-2025 period. Fellows for Cohort 2 were selected by the Expert Selection Committee and were subsequently onboarded into the program.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others healthtech]
A pre-immersion program was conducted by Dr. Avijit Bansal (AIIMS Delhi, Stanford India Biodesign) in collaboration with the SIIC team, designed to prepare the fellows ahead of their clinical immersion. In June 2024, the Cohort 2 fellows-comprising two teams-underwent clinical immersion at SCTIMST, Trivandrum, where they engaged directly with real-world healthcare environments to identify pressing clinical challenges.
The following are the two selected teams:
Team 1: Harsh Arora and Sheersha Karmakar - Developing an affordable Sedation & brain oxygenation monitoring device under the mentorship of Prof. Arjun Ramakrishnan.
Team 2: Akshat Chouksey, Lakshit Sharma, and C. Zoliansangi - Developing an assistive bowel management device under the mentorship of Prof. Amitabha Bandyopadhyay.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others healthtech]
Following this phase, the HII Sync event provided a platform for expert clinical and engineering mentorship. This support was instrumental in refining and finalizing the problem statements that the teams would address. The fellows then progressed to the mentorship and prototyping stages, with financial support allocated for prototype development.
To further promote entrepreneurial skills, the program hosted expert sessions, including workshops on “Design Thinking” and “Mastering Finance and Legal Compliance for the sustainable growth of startups”.
Programmes and Events
The following are the finalized Problem Statements:
Team 1: Harsh Arora and Sheersha Karmakar
Project Title: Design of a device for monitoring sedation level and cerebral oxygenation in critical care settings.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others healthtech]
This addresses the critical gap in continuous and objective monitoring of neurological function during sedation in ICUs and operation theatres.
Team 2: Akshat Chouksey, Lakshit Sharma, and C. Zoliansangi
Project Title: Development of an assistive device for individuals with conditions such as Imperforate Anus, Hirschsprung’s Disease, Paralysis, and Spinal Injuries.
The aim is to support bowel management and improve quality of life through user-friendly, low-burden interventions.
Prototyping (ongoing)
Following the finalization of problem statements, fellows have commenced prototyping their proposed solutions. Utilizing research and analytical facilities at IIT Kanpur, they are translating clinical insights into tangible innovations, supported by dedicated prototype development funding.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others healthtech]
Future direction
Following the successful implementation of Cohorts 1 and 2, the HII Program is ready to onboard the third cohort.
Conclusion
The second cohort of the HII Program has consistently made significant progress. From the initial pre-immersion phase through clinical immersion, the identification of critical healthcare challenges, and the development of innovative prototypes, the fellows have exhibited dedication, ingenuity, and a strong grasp of real-world clinical needs.
The efforts undertaken thus far have established a solid foundation for future advancements and impact. Supported by expert mentorship, access to cutting-edge facilities, and dedicated resources, the fellows are well-equipped to translate their ideas into viable healthcare solutions. Their journey embodies the core mission of the HII Program: to bridge the gap between clinical challenges and technological innovation.

=== new initiatives others humanoidclub ===

OTHER INITIATIVES
Humanoid Club IITK
The Humanoid-Robot project or HURO as we call it, is an effort by the students of IITK to enter into and make a contribution to the world of autonomous robotics. The team aims to make a humanoid robot capable of performing various pre-determined athletic tasks as required by the FIRA-HURO cup.
Achievements in the year 2023-24 include:
Qualification in FIRA HuroCup Finals 2024
We were able to successfully qualify for the FIRA RoboWorldCup Finals held in São Luís, Brazil, making us the Sole Indian Representative at this prestigious event. The FIRA Hurocup, one of the world's leading robotics competitions, brought together over 360 teams from 22 countries, showcasing talent in the field of robotics and artificial intelligence.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others humanoidclub]
Our bot was designed to excel in a variety of tasks that were part of the competition, including:
Archery: Where precision and accuracy were crucial as our bot aimed and fired arrows at designated targets.
Weightlifting: This task tested the bot's mechanical strength and balance as it lifted weights of varying sizes.
Basketball: A challenge that required our bot to demonstrate agility and control by shooting basketballs into hoops.
The event, held from August 5 to August 9, 2024, hosted by the Multicenter Sebrae in São Luís, the competition provided a platform for teams to exchange knowledge. The qualification in the top 10 teams worldwide is a significant milestone that highlights the potential of our team and the support we have received from Noccarc.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others humanoidclub]
IEEE ICRA Wrestling League 2023
Team Humanoid proudly secured 4th place on the worldwide leader board in the first stage of the IEEE ICRA Wrestling League 2023. This prestigious event brought together top robotics teams globally, challenging them to design and program robots capable of complex wrestling manoeuvres.
Team Humanoid demonstrated our excellence by achieving 2nd place globally in the rigorous qualifiers for the IEEE IROS Humanoid Wrestling Competition 2023. This prestigious competition challenges teams to push the limits of robotics, requiring advanced design, programming, and strategic thinking to succeed in humanoid wrestling tasks.
“We would like to extend our deepest gratitude to Noccarc Robotics for their unwavering support and generous sponsorship throughout the year.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others humanoidclub]
Your belief in our vision has empowered us to explore new frontiers, push the boundaries of technology, and make meaningful contributions to the industry. We are truly honored to have you as our partner in this journey, and we look forward to continuing this fruitful collaboration in the years to come.
This report reflects not only our achievements but also the trust and confidence that Noccarc Robotics has placed in us. We are excited about the future and remain committed to delivering exceptional results that align with our shared values and goals.
Thank you for your invaluable support, without which our progress would not have been possible”.

=== new initiatives others iitkmotorsports ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/others/iitkMotorsports.php
OTHER INITIATIVES
IITK Motorsports
IITK Motorsports is a team of passionate, driven, and dedicated students united by the goal of building a 'Racecar'. Comprising members from diverse departments and years, the team thrives on exploring and learning in the automotive domain. Founded in 2010 by students eager to apply classroom knowledge to real-world engineering, the team has competed in prestigious events like Formula Student Italy, Formula Bharat, SUPRA, and Mega ATV Championship since 2011. Most recently, at Formula Imperial 2024, IITK Motorsports emerged as the Overall EV Champions and once again brought pride and honour to the institute.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others iitkmotorsports]
Development in the FY 2024-25
The team was committed to designing and building a top-performing electric vehicle for Formula Bharat, focusing on optimizing performance to maximize points while also upgrading their car from the previous tenure for Formula Imperial. Each component, from the accumulator to the suspension and braking systems, was carefully designed, tested, and integrated.
Advanced tools such as SolidWorks for 3D design, OptimumLap for performance simulation, and ANSYS for structural and aerodynamic analysis were used. Lotus Shark and MATLAB supported suspension analysis, while Altium Designer and MicroCap were used for electronics and circuit design. These tools enabled data-driven optimization and ensured a competitive vehicle.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others iitkmotorsports]
The team has built two Formula Student electric vehicles and achieved several milestones, including:
Overall EV Champions, Formula Imperial 2024
Best Innovation and Best Engineering Design Awards, Formula Imperial 2024
1st Place Overall, Formula Bharat 2023 (Virtual)
9th Place, Formula Bharat 2025
The team is currently developing its third electric vehicle with further improvements and innovations.
The project provided students with valuable hands-on engineering experience and strengthened IIT Kanpur’s presence in national and international competitions. It also promoted sustainable mobility and inspired innovation within the campus community.
The team sincerely thanks Bright Four Wheel Sales Private Limited for their generous support. Their contribution was instrumental in the successful development and participation of the Formula Student Electric Vehicle. The team deeply appreciates their trust and continued encouragement.

=== new initiatives others surge ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/others/surge.php
OTHER INITIATIVES
SURGE - Students-Undergraduate Research and Graduate Excellence Program
SURGE is an internship Program of IIT Kanpur, which runs under the aegis of Office of Outreach Activities (OOA), IIT Kanpur. SURGE program was launched and inaugurated on 8th May 2006, it aims to promote undergraduate research and interdisciplinary learning through close student–faculty collaboration.
Open to students from IIT Kanpur, other institutions in India, and SAARC countries, SURGE provides hands-on research experience and a platform for innovative ideas. Several projects have led to publications in reputed international journals and conferences, inspiring participants to pursue research careers. The program also recognizes excellence through awards for Best Poster and Best Project in Engineering and Sciences, including the Dr. Elizabeth and Dr. Verkey Cherian Awards.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others surge]
The SURGE-2024 program was successfully conducted in offline mode, providing a dynamic platform for undergraduate research and innovation. A total of 325 students were selected for the program and completed their internships under the guidance of IIT Kanpur faculty mentors.
The selected participants included:
187 from IIT Kanpur
135 from Non-IITK institutions
3 from SAARC nations (Nepal)
In addition to research work, a lecture series was organized during the program. Distinguished speakers from diverse fields were invited to deliver talks on contemporary and interdisciplinary topics.
A Poster Presentation was held on 10-11 July 2024, where participants showcased their research findings, evaluated by an IIT Kanpur faculty committee.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others surge]
The program concluded with a Farewell and Certificate Distribution Ceremony on 12 July 2024, chaired by Prof. Braj Bhushan (Deputy Director) in the presence of Prof. A. R. Harish (Dean, R&D) and Prof. Kantesh Balani (Dean, Resources and Alumni).
To honor exceptional contributions, 8 participants were selected for awards under various categories. These awards served to encourage and recognize the outstanding efforts and research achievements of the interns.
We sincerely thank DoRA and all our donors and alumni for their invaluable support. Their contributions enabled financial assistance for 60 students under the Institute Funded category and supported the awards and logistics of the program. This support ensured that meritorious students could participate regardless of financial background while helping maintain the high standards of the SURGE program. We look forward to your continued support in nurturing the next generation of researchers and innovators.

=== new initiatives others toastmasters ===
Source: https://www.iitk.ac.in/dora/new_initiatives/others/toastmasters.php
OTHER INITIATIVES
Toastmasters Club
The Toastmasters Club at IIT Kanpur, supported by our esteemed alumnus Mr. Suresh Bazaj (MSc2/PHY/1971), works in close coordination with the Students’ Placement Office to help students enhance their public speaking, communication, and leadership skills. It is a student-led, self-learning initiative where members participate in regular meetings, workshops, and leadership training sessions designed to boost confidence, articulation, and professional readiness.
From orientation programs for new students to advanced workshops on impromptu speaking, phonetics, and interview preparation, the club offers a structured and engaging platform for personality development. With the formation of a fifth club this year and consistent participation in campus and off-campus sessions, the initiative continues to grow. Over 100 students braved the winter chill to attend a power-packed session on interview readiness and confidence building led by DTM Deepak Menon, while Dr. Garima Williams conducted an engaging session on phonetics, helping students sharpen their speech clarity and professional communication. IIT Kanpur was once again recognised with the prestigious “Communication & Leadership Award” by Toastmasters International - the only institute in the subcontinent to earn this honour.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others toastmasters]
Milestones & Achievements in FY 2024 -25
FORMATION OF CLUB 5 - TOASTMASTERS CLUB IITK#5
Over the past few months, Toastmasters Clubs IITK have successfully conducted more than 50 sessions of Toastmasters International. After the successful running of 04 clubs and promotional activities, a large no. of students showed their interest in joining the Toastmasters club. This led to the formation of a fifth club at IITK.
THE COMMUNICATION AND LEADERSHIP AWARD TO IITK FROM TOASTMASTERS INTERNATIONAL
IIT Kanpur was awarded the 'Communication & Leadership Award' once again for its outstanding contribution towards the District and Toastmasters International. The Communication and Leadership Award is presented by the district to a corporate or an institution in the community that has contributed to the cause of the District and Toastmasters International.This award is given for the first time to an institution in the subcontinent.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others toastmasters]

Toastmasters International conducted an online club officers training program for all club officers. Students who participated got insights about how to lead club meetings. There were leaders from industry who trained the participants on their respective roles in Toastmasters club.
ORIENTATION PROGRAMME CONDUCTED FOR NEW JEE STUDENTS TO SHARE BENEFITS OF TOASTMASTERS IN THEIR CAREERS
Students attended the workshop in large numbers and joined the meetings to improve their inter-personnel skills. This workshop was conducted on Aug 10, 2024 and a total of 90 students attended the session and thereby joined Toastmasters club formally.
STUDENTS LEVERAGED THE BENEFITS OF TOASTMASTERS INTERNATIONAL

Students also attended a Sunday morning meeting in Kanpur city to gain experience speaking in front of an unfamiliar audience in an unfamiliar meeting room. Regular participation in Toastmasters meetings on campus and off campus is an indication of how Toastmasters is helping students with their communication skills
TRAINING ON VARIOUS TOASTMASTERS MODULES BY DTM DEEPAK MENON
An interactive jam-packed session on interview readiness and confidence building was arranged. More than 100 students participated. Students got many insights on impromptu speaking and effectively giving positive feedback. This event was led by Toastmasters past International President, DTM Deepak Menon.
[Context: IIT KANPUR DORA - GIVING BACK: FUNDS, SCHOLARSHIPS, CSR, CENTRES AND INITIATIVES > new initiatives others toastmasters]
Students were given sessions on Pathways - the education curriculum of Toastmasters. The Pathways’ learning experience is an interactive and flexible education program created to help students strengthen their communication and leadership skills.
WORKSHOP ON PHONETICS TO MASTER PRONUNCIATION
Students of all the clubs attended weekly meetings and participated in meeting roles. Students also attended a Sunday morning meeting in Kanpur city to gain experience speaking in front of an unfamiliar audience in an unfamiliar meeting room.
